# Frontline content catalogue

Generated by `pnpm catalog:doc`, do not edit by hand; edit the source file named under each heading and regenerate.

## Removed: the "Ground pays" tag on the mission board

The mission board used to carry a tag at the right of the "Board 1 of N" row reading "Ground pays +N%" (or "Standing rate" when the bonus was zero). It was the area’s pay premium: every district pays a percentage more for the same job than the misc board does, 9 percentage points per point of district difficulty above 1 (`PAY_PERCENT_PER_DIFFICULTY` in `packages/shared/src/missions.areas.ts`), plus 10 points per crew level above the first (`PAY_PERCENT_PER_LEVEL`) and any spoils bonus from the crew’s standing. The number is still applied to every reward on the board and frozen onto a mission at launch; only the tag that quoted it on the board header has been removed (maintainer request, 2026-09-10). The per-card "expected haul" already shows the scaled figures.

| `bp_greenhouse_retrofit` | Greenhouse Retrofit Blueprint | BASIC | 720 | no | Glazing bars and an irrigation loop that does not need anybody standing over it. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 2 pages. |
| `bp_generator_retrofit` | Generator Retrofit Blueprint | INTRICATE | 1620 | no | Winding diagrams and a governor linkage, for the machine everything else is plugged into. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_scrapyard_retrofit` | Scrapyard Retrofit Blueprint | BASIC | 1620 | no | A sorting line and press tooling. The yard stops being a heap and becomes a shop. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_apothecary_retrofit` | Apothecary Retrofit Blueprint | INTRICATE | 1620 | no | A still column and dosage tables somebody died working out. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_gate_retrofit` | Gate Retrofit Blueprint | INTRICATE | 1620 | no | Counterweights and bar sockets. It has to shut faster than anybody can run. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_lab_retrofit` | Lab Retrofit Blueprint | ADVANCED | 2880 | no | Bench layout, an extraction hood and a room clean enough to be worth the trouble. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 4 pages. |
| `bp_gauntlet_retrofit` | Gauntlet Retrofit Blueprint | INTRICATE | 2880 | no | Obstacle frames, drainage under the sand, and a board everyone can read their score off. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 4 pages. |
| `bp_infirmary_retrofit` | Infirmary Retrofit Blueprint | INTRICATE | 1620 | no | Ward layout and a sterile line people actually keep to. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_garage_retrofit` | Garage Retrofit Blueprint | INTRICATE | 2880 | no | Pit layout, hoist ratings and a parts wall with everything where it should be. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 4 pages. |
| `bp_overnight_plating` | Overnight Plating Blueprint | BASIC | 720 | no | A cut list and a weld sequence, for the night before rather than the month before. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 2 pages. |
| `bp_shaped_charges` | Shaped Charge Blueprint | INTRICATE | 1620 | no | Cone geometry and a standoff table. Cut for this wall, this week. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_approach_plans` | Approach Plans Blueprint | BASIC | 720 | no | Somebody surveyed the doors and wrote down which way the specialists go in. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 2 pages. |
| `bp_refined_accelerant` | Refined Accelerant Blueprint | ADVANCED | 2880 | no | Fuel nobody should be able to make, and four pages on how not to be standing near it. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 4 pages. |
| `bp_pressure_plates` | Pressure Plate Blueprint | BASIC | 720 | no | Which boards to lift, what to put under them, and how much weight sets it off. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 2 pages. |
| `bp_buried_shell` | Buried Shell Blueprint | INTRICATE | 1620 | no | How to move a cracked chemical round, how deep to put it, and where the wire runs. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_prepared_collapse` | Prepared Collapse Blueprint | ADVANCED | 2880 | no | A survey of what is holding the frontage up, and the order in which to stop it. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 4 pages. |
| `bp_razor_wire` | Razor Wire Blueprint | BASIC | 720 | no | How to draw tape off a reel without losing a hand, and where to peg it down. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 2 pages. |
| `bp_fuel_fougasse` | Fuel Fougasse Blueprint | INTRICATE | 1620 | no | A drum on its side in a pit, the angle it is dug at, and the charge behind it. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |
| `bp_flooded_cellar` | Flooded Cellar Blueprint | ADVANCED | 1620 | no | Which cellar to fill, where the water goes when it is let go, and what is in it. | Unlocked, permanently. Assembled from 3 pages. |

| `boost_call_in_the_name` | Call In The Name | 200 | +12% attack for everything you send | Anybody | Every debt the street owes you, called in at once and spent on this one night. |
| `boost_stand_your_ground` | Stand Your Ground | 200 | +15% defence for everything you send | Anybody | Word goes out that anybody who runs tonight does not come back to this district. |
| `boost_make_an_example` | Make An Example | 320 | +20% morale for everything you send | Anybody | Something public, something ugly, and nobody on your side thinking about home. |
| `boost_paid_in_advance` | Paid In Advance | 620 | +40% attack for your rabble | Proposed by your Head of Finance | The cheap end of the roster, paid before the fight instead of after it. They notice. |
| `boost_drilled_all_week` | Drilled All Week | 420 | +28% attack for your engineered units | Proposed by your Instructor of the Young | Seven days of the same approach, walked until nobody has to be told twice. |
| `boost_the_right_doors` | The Right Doors | 560 | +30% attack for your specialists | Proposed by your Master of Whispers | Somebody has already been inside and marked which way the specialists go in. |
| `boost_plated_overnight` | Plated Overnight | 700 | +35% defence for your heavy units | Proposed by the Lab: Standard Parts | Every heavy thing you own, up on blocks and welded to until the sun came up. |
| `boost_shaped_for_this` | Shaped For This | 640 | +32% attack for your heavy units | Proposed by the Lab: Shaped Charges | The charges cut for this wall, this week, by somebody who measured it. |
| `boost_they_came_for_this` | They Came For This | 780 | +45% attack for your legends | Proposed by your Raid Boss | The one on your roster the city tells stories about, told the story is tonight. |
| `boost_the_colossus_walks` | The Colossus Walks | 1150 | +50% attack for The Colossus | Proposed by the Lab: Demolition Doctrine | Fuel nobody should be able to get, poured into the biggest thing in the city. |

## Building modifications

Source: `packages/shared/src/building/modifications.ts`, `packages/shared/src/building/addons.ts`

An advanced modification is one worth 12 points or more: it costs high quality metal on top of the scrap, and it sits behind the structure’s retrofit blueprint. Prices are list prices; the Scrapyard takes 2% off per level above its first, to a cap of 30%, and each entry opens at the yard level shown (`packages/shared/src/building/scrapyard.ts`).

## Research

Source: `packages/shared/src/research/tracks.ts`

18 tracks of 10 rungs. Cost and minutes come from the rung's depth, not from the row: see `researchItemCost` and `researchItemMinutes`. Mark is what the chair holding the track needs; head mark is what the Head of Research needs alongside.

#### Master of Whispers (10)

What you find out, and what it costs to.

| Step | Id | Name | Payout | Cost | Minutes | Mark | Head mark | Description |

| 2 | `tech_paid_informants` | Paid Informants | +6% intel | 1550 Caps, 950 Scrap | 70 | F | - | A few caps a week to people who see things, and a few more when they see something. |

## Training drills

Source: `packages/shared/src/crew/training.ts`

| Attribute | Title | Detail |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Strength | Axle work | A truck axle, a chalk line and somebody counting badly. |
| Stamina | The stair run | Forty flights of the Nexus service stack, then forty more, breathing the smog. |
| Dexterity | Wire drill | Strip, splice, seal. Blindfolded by the end of the hour, because the power stays on. |
| Speed | Curfew sprints | Marked distance across the yard, on the clock, on the whistle. |
| Reflexes | The drop board | Somebody drops a bolt without warning. You catch it or you pick it up. |
| Toughness | Cold hours | Stand in the vent wash until it stops being interesting, then keep standing. |
| Stealth | Floor plan walk | Cross a lit room without a single person in it looking up. |
| Organization | The board | Take a stalled job apart on paper until the reason it stalled is on one line. |
| Analysis | After-action reading | A stack of reports on fights that went wrong, and no help about which part mattered. |
| Improvisation | Wrong-parts bench | Build the thing on the card out of the crate, which does not contain the parts. |
| Logic | Three witnesses | Three accounts of the same night. Two are wrong and nobody says which. |
| Composure | Breath and count | An hour of doing something dull while the alarm bell is rung on purpose. |
| Resolve | The long sit | Hold a position nobody is contesting, past the point it feels like a waste. |
| Intuition | Cold room | Walk into a room somebody left in a hurry and say what happened in it. |
| Strategy | The map table | Take the district apart into ground worth holding and ground worth losing. |
| Authority | Standing the room | Give a briefing to people who have decided in advance not to be impressed. |
| Leadership | Shift handover | Run the change of watch. Nobody leaves confused, nobody leaves resentful. |
| Charisma | A night at the bar | Buy nothing, leave with four names and a favour owed. |
| Communication | Radio discipline | Say the whole thing in nine words, over a channel that keeps dropping. |
| Intimidation | The doorway | Stand in one. Practise not saying anything at all. |
| Negotiation | Wage table | Open the book with somebody who has done this longer than you have. |
| Deception | The false ledger | Write a week of records that survive being read carefully by a stranger. |
| Empathy | The quiet one | Find whoever has stopped talking this week and find out what about. |
| Diplomacy | Neighbour's table | An hour with a crew you are not fighting, spent not starting anything. |
| Engineering | Teardown | Strip a generator to its plates and have it running before the shift ends. |
| Signals | Combine traffic | A day of intercepts, most of it weather reports, one of it not. Then the same pass over y… |
| Craft | Bench fit | Make the part twice. Keep the one that seats without persuasion, and mend the one that di… |
| Medicine | Triage round | The infirmary at shift change, deciding who waits. |
| Cybernetics | Calibration | Tune somebody else's shunt while they tell you exactly how it feels. |
| Salvage | Wreck walk | An hour in the yard deciding what is worth the trip and what is scenery. |
| Encyclopedia | Reading week | Half a shelf of somebody else’s trade, at speed. Nobody is examined on it and everybody i… |
| Navigation | Undergrid route | Cross four levels without surfacing and without asking anyone. |
| Chemistry | The bathtub run | Same feedstock, better yield, nothing catches fire. |
| Logistics | Manifest night | Count the warehouse against the book until they agree. |
| Cryptography | Cipher hour | Break yesterday's traffic, then write today's so it cannot be. |

## Notoriety tiers

Source: `packages/shared/src/economy/notoriety.ts`

| Rung | Tier | What the street is doing |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Nobody | Nobody has heard of you, which is its own kind of safety. |
| 1 | Unknown | A few people could describe you. None of them would bother. |
| 2 | Ill-Reputed | Your name comes up, and never in a sentence anybody enjoys. |
| 3 | Back-Alley Rumored | Stories about you have started, and half of them are wrong. |
| 4 | Whispered | People lower their voices. That is new. |
| 5 | Marked | The Combine has a file, and somebody senior has read it. |
| 6 | Known Trouble | Doors are shut before you reach them. Some of them stay shut. |
| 7 | Bad Omen | Crews call off work because you were seen in the district. |
| 8 | Feared | Nobody negotiates with you from a position they have not already conceded. |
| 9 | Dreaded | The word for you is used on children, and it works. |
| 10 | Street Devil | What you are is no longer discussed as a person. |
| 11 | Scourge | Districts you have never entered plan around you. |
| 12 | Nightmare | The city does not argue about whether the stories are true. |
| 13 | Nameless | Nobody says it out loud any more. That is what the top of this looks like. |
